sidebar)\n505: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n506: pub enum WorkspaceMsg {\n507:     /// Toggle sidebar visibility (Cmd+B)\n508:     ToggleSidebar,\n509: \n510:     /// Toggle folder expanded/collapsed state\n511:     ToggleFolder(std::path::PathBuf),\n512: \n513:     /// Expand a folder\n514:     ExpandFolder(std::path::PathBuf),\n515: \n516:     /// Collapse a folder\n517:     CollapseFolder(std::path::PathBuf),\n518: \n519:     /// Select an item in the file tree\n520:     SelectItem(std::path::PathBuf),\n521: \n522:     /// Select previous item in file tree (Arrow Up)\n523:     SelectPrevious,\n524: \n525:     /// Select next item in file tree (Arrow Down)\n526:     SelectNext,\n527: \n528:     /// Select parent folder of current item (Arrow Left on collapsed/file)\n529:     SelectParent,\n530: \n531:     /// Open file from tree (single-click = preview, double-click = permanent)\n532:     OpenFile {\n533:         path: std::path::PathBuf,\n534:         preview: bool,\n535:     },\n536: \n537:     /// Open selected file or toggle folder (Enter key behavior)\n538:     OpenOrToggle,\n539: \n540:     /// Reveal the active file in the tree (Cmd+Shift+R)\n541:     RevealActiveFile,\n542: \n543:     /// Start resizing sidebar\n544:     StartSidebarResize { initial_x: f64 },\n545: \n546:     /// Update sidebar width during resize\n547:     UpdateSidebarResize { x: f64 },\n548: \n549:     /// End sidebar resize\n550:     EndSidebarResize,\n551: \n552:     /// Refresh the file tree from disk\n553:     Refresh,\n554: \n555:     /// Scroll the sidebar file tree (positive = down, negative = up)\n556:     Scroll { lines: i32 },\n557: \n558:     /// File system change detected by watcher (triggers tree refresh)\n559:     /// Contains the paths that changed for incremental updates.\n560:     FileSystemChange { paths: Vec<PathBuf> },\n561: }\n562: \n563: /// Top-level message type\n564: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n565: pub enum Msg {\n566:     /// Editor messages (cursor, viewport)\n567:     Editor(EditorMsg),\n568:     /// Document messages (text editing)\n569:     Document(DocumentMsg),\n570:     /// UI messages (status, animation)\n571:     Ui(UiMsg),\n572:     /// Layout messages (splits, tabs, groups)\n573:     Layout(LayoutMsg),\n574:     /// App messages (file I/O, window)\n575:     App(AppMsg),\n576:     /// Syntax highlighting messages\n577:     Syntax(SyntaxMsg),\n578:     /// CSV mode messages\n579:     Csv(CsvMsg),\n580:     /// Workspace messages (file tree)\n581:     Workspace(WorkspaceMsg),\n582:     /// Unified text editing messages (Phase 2 - editable system)\n583:     TextEdit(EditContext, TextEditMsg),\n584: }\n585: \n586: // Convenience constructors for common messages\n587: impl Msg {\n588:     /// Create a cursor movement message\n589:     pub fn move_cursor(direction: Direction) -> Self {\n590:         Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::MoveCursor(direction))\n591:     }\n592: \n593:     /// Create an insert character message\n594:     pub fn insert_char(ch: char) -> Self {\n595:         Msg::Document(DocumentMsg::InsertChar(ch))\n596:     }\n597: \n598:     /// Create a resize message\n599:     pub fn resize(width: u32, height: u32) -> Self {\n600:         Msg::App(AppMsg::Resize(width, height))\n601:     }\n602: }",

This module implements a hierarchical layout system for multiple editor panes,\n4: //! tabs, and split views, allowing the same document to be viewed in multiple places.\n5: \n6: use std::collections::HashMap;\n7: \n8: use super::document::Document;\n9: use super::editor::{EditorState, ScrollRevealMode};\n10: \n11: // ============================================================================\n12: // Identifiers\n13: // ============================================================================\n14: \n15: /// Unique identifier for a document\n16: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]\n17: pub struct DocumentId(pub u64);\n18: \n19: /// Unique identifier for an editor view\n20: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]\n21: pub struct EditorId(pub u64);\n22: \n23: /// Unique identifier for an editor group (pane)\n24: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]\n25: pub struct GroupId(pub u64);\n26: \n27: /// Unique identifier for a tab\n28: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]\n29: pub struct TabId(pub u64);\n30: \n31: // ============================================================================\n32: // Layout Primitives\n33: // ============================================================================\n34: \n35: /// Rectangle for layout calculations\n36: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Default)]\n37: pub struct Rect {\n38:     pub x: f32,\n39:     pub y: f32,\n40:     pub width: f32,\n41:     pub height: f32,\n42: }\n43: \n44: impl Rect {\n45:     pub fn new(x: f32, y: f32, width: f32, height: f32) -> Self {\n46:         Self {\n47:             x,\n48:             y,\n49:             width,\n50:             height,\n51:         }\n52:     }\n53: \n54:     pub fn contains(&self, px: f32, py: f32) -> bool {\n55:         px >= self.x && px < self.x + self.width && py >= self.y && py < self.y + self.height\n56:     }\n57: }\n58: \n59: // ============================================================================\n60: // Tabs\n61: // ============================================================================\n62: \n63: /// A tab represents an open editor in a group\n64: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n65: pub struct Tab {\n66:     pub Rect,\n88: }\n89: \n90: impl EditorGroup {\n91:     /// Get the currently active tab\n92:     pub fn active_tab(&self) -> Option<&Tab> {\n93:         self.tabs.get(self.active_tab_index)\n94:     }\n95: \n96:     /// Get the editor ID of the active tab\n97:     pub fn active_editor_id(&self) -> Option<EditorId> {\n98:         self.active_tab().map(|t| t.editor_id)\n99:     }\n100: }\n101: \n102: // ============================================================================\n103: // Layout Tree\n104: // ============================================================================\n105: \n106: /// Direction for splitting editor groups\n107: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]\n108: pub enum SplitDirection {\n109:     /// Children arranged left-to-right\n110:     Horizontal,\n111:     /// Children arranged top-to-bottom\n112:     Vertical,\n113: }\n114: \n115: /// A container that splits space between children\n116: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n117: pub struct SplitContainer {\n118:     pub direction: SplitDirection,\n119:     pub children: Vec<LayoutNode>,\n120:     /// Proportional sizes (0.0 to 1.0, must sum to 1.0)\n121:     pub ratios: Vec<f32>,\n122:     /// Minimum size in pixels for each child\n123:     // TODO: min_sizes is currently not enforced in compute_layout_node().\n124:     // Implement enforcement to prevent panes from being resized below usable size.\n125:     // This is a UX enhancement, not a crash risk (Rust bounds checking handles edge cases).\n126:     pub min_sizes: Vec<f32>,\n127: }\n128: \n129: /// A node in the layout tree - either a group or a split container\n130: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n131: pub enum LayoutNode {\n132:     Group(GroupId),\n133:     Split(SplitContainer),\n134: }\n135: \n136: // struct EditorArea {\n143:     /// All open documents (shared across editors)\n144:     pub documents: HashMap<DocumentId, Document>,\n145: \n146:     /// All editor states (each references a The layout tree root\n153:     pub layout: LayoutNode,\n154: \n155:     /// Currently focused group\n156:     pub focused_group_id: GroupId,\n157: \n158:     /// ID generators\n159:     next_document_id: u64,\n160:     next_editor_id: u64,\n161:     next_group_id: u64,\n162:     next_tab_id: u64,\n163: \n164:     /// Counter for generating unique untitled document names\n165: GroupId(1);\n178:         let tab_id = TabId(1);\n179: \n180:         // Assign IDs to document and editor\n181:         document.id = Some(doc_id);\n182:         editor.id = Some(editor_id);\n183: {\n202:                 id: group_id,\n203:                 tabs: vec![tab],\n204:                 active_tab_index: 0,\n205:                 rect: Rect::default(),\n206:             },\n207:         );\n208: \n209:         Self {\n210:             documents,\n211:             editors,\n212:             groups,\n213:             layout: LayoutNode::Group(group_id),\n214: next_untitled_number: 1,\n220:             last_layout_rect: None,\n221:         }\n222:     }\n223: \n224:     /// Get the currently focused group\n225:     pub fn focused_group(&self) -> pub fn focused_editor_id(&self) -> Option<EditorId> {\n242:         self.focused_group().and_then(|g| g.active_editor_id())\n243:     }\n244: \n245:     /// Get the focused editor state\n246:     pub }\n515:             LayoutNode::Split(container) => {\n516:                 let children = &container.children;\n517:                 let ratios = &container.ratios;\n518: \n519:                 if children.is_empty() {\n520:                     return;\n521:                 }\n522: \n523:                 // Calculate child rects based on direction and ratios\n524:                 let mut offset child_size;\n577:                 }\n578:             }\n579:         }\n580:     }\n581: \n582:     /// Find the group at a given point (for mouse clicks)\n583:     pub fn group_at_point(&self, x: f32, y: f32) -> Option<GroupId> {\n584:         self.group_at_point_node(&self.layout, x, y)\n585:     }\n586: \n587:     /// Recursively search for group at point\n588:     fn group_at_point_node(&self, node: &LayoutNode, x: f32, y: f32) -> Option<GroupId> {\n589:         match node {\n590:             LayoutNode::Group(group_id) => {\n591:                 if let Some(group) = self.groups.get(group_id) {\n592:                     if group.rect.contains(x, y) {\n593:                         return Some(*group_id);\n594:                     }\n595: }\n606:         }\n607:     }\n608: \n609:     /// Find splitter bar at a given point (for drag handling)\n610:     pub fn splitter_at_point(&self, splitters: &[SplitterBar], x: f32, y: f32) -> Option<usize> {\n611:         for (i, splitter) in splitters.iter().enumerate() {\n612:             if splitter.rect.contains(x, y) {\n613:                 return Some(i);\n614:             }\n615: ============================================================================\n623: \n624: /// Width of splitter bars in pixels\n625: pub const SPLITTER_WIDTH: f32 = 6.0;\n626: \n627: /// Adjust a cursor/selection position based on an edit that occurred.\n628: ///\n629: /// This is used to synchronize cursors across multiple views of the same document.\n630: /// When an edit happens at (edit_line, edit_column):\n631: /// - If the cursor is before the edit point: no change\n632: /// - If the cursor is on the same line, at or after the edit column: adjust column\n633: /// - If the cursor is on a later line: adjust line number\n634: fn adjust_position_for_edit(\n635:     pos_line: &mut usize,\n636:     pos_column: &mut usize,\n637:     edit_line: usize,\n638:     edit_column: usize,\n639:     lines_delta: isize,\n640:     column_delta: isize,\n641: ) {\n642:     if *pos_line < edit_line {\n643:         // Position is before the edit line - no adjustment needed\n644:         return;\n645:     }\n646: \n647:     if *pos_line == edit_line {\n648:         // Same line as edit\n649:         if *pos_column >= edit_column {\n650:             // At or after edit column - adjust column\n651:             if column_delta >= 0 {\n652:                 *pos_column = pos_column.saturating_add(column_delta as usize);\n653:             } else {\n654:                 *pos_column = pos_column.saturating_sub((-column_delta) as usize);\n655:             }\n656: \n657:             // If lines were added/removed, we might need to adjust both\n658:             if lines_delta > 0 {\n659:                 // Newline was inserted - cursor moves to new line\n660:                 // Column becomes: pos_column - edit_column (position on new line)\n661:                 *pos_line = pos_line.saturating_add(lines_delta as usize);\n662:                 *pos_column = pos_column.saturating_sub(edit_column);\n663:             } else if lines_delta < 0 {\n664:                 // Lines were joined - no line adjustment for same-line positions\n665:             }\n666:         }\n667:         // Before edit column on same line - no adjustment\n668:     } else {\n669:         // Position is on a line after the edit line\n670:         if lines_delta >= 0 {\n671:             *pos_line = pos_line.saturating_add(lines_delta as usize);\n672:         } else {\n673:             *pos_line = pos_line.saturating_sub((-lines_delta) as usize);\n674:         }\n675:     }\n676: }\n677: \n678: /// Represents a draggable splitter bar between editor groups\n679: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]\n680: pub struct SplitterBar {\n681:     /// Direction of the split this bar controls\n682:     pub direction: SplitDirection,\n683:     /// The hit-testing rectangle for this splitter\n684:     pub rect: Rect,\n685:     /// Index of this splitter within its parent container\n686:     pub index: usize,\n687: }\n688: \n689: // ============================================================================\n690: // Debug Invariant Validation\n691: // ============================================================================\n692: \n693: impl EditorArea {\n694:     /// Validate internal invariants in debug builds.\n695:     ///\n696:     /// This function checks that:\n697:     /// - focused_group_id points to an existing group\n698:     /// - All groups have valid active_tab_index values\n699:     /// - All tabs reference existing editors\n700:     /// - All editors reference existing documents (if document_id is Some)\n701:     ///\n702:     /// Panics in debug builds if any invariant is violated.\n703:     #[cfg(debug_assertions)]\n704:     pub fn assert_invariants(&self) {\n705:         // Check focused group exists\n706:         assert!(\n707:             self.groups.contains_key(&self.focused_group_id),\n708:             \"focused_group_id {:?} does not exist in groups\",\n709:             self.focused_group_id\n710:         );\n711: \n712:         // Check each group\n713:         for (group_id, group) in &self.groups {\n714:             // Check active_tab_index is valid\n715:             if !group.tabs.is_empty() {\n716:                 assert!(\n717:                     group.active_tab_index < group.tabs.len(),\n718:                     \"Group {:?} has active_tab_index {} but only {} tabs\",\n719:                     group_id,\n720:                     group.active_tab_index,\n721:                     group.tabs.len()\n722:                 );\n723:             }\n724: \n725:             // Check each tab references a valid editor\n726:             for tab in &group.tabs {\n727:                 assert!(\n728:                     self.editors.contains_key(&tab.editor_id),\n729:                     \"Tab {:?} references non-existent editor {:?}\",\n730:                     tab.id,\n731:                     tab.editor_id\n732:                 );\n733:             }\n734:         }\n735: \n736:         // Check each editor references a valid document (if document_id is Some)\n737:         for (editor_id, editor) in &self.editors {\n738:             if let Some(doc_id) = editor.document_id {\n739:                 assert!(\n740:                     self.documents.contains_key(&doc_id),\n741:                     \"Editor {:?} references non-existent document {:?}\",\n742:                     editor_id,\n743:                     doc_id\n744:                 );\n745:             }\n746:         }\n747:     }\n748: \n749:     /// No-op in release builds\n750:     #[cfg(not(debug_assertions))]\n751:     #[inline]\n752:     pub fn assert_invariants(&self) {}\n753: }",

{
  "content": "1: //! Theme system for the editor\n2: //!\n3: //! Provides YAML-based theming support with compile-time embedded themes\n4: //! and user-defined themes from config directories.\n5: //!\n6: //! Theme loading priority:\n7: //! 1. User config: `~/.config/token-editor/themes/{id}.yaml`\n8: //! 2. Embedded: Built-in themes compiled into binary\n9: \n10: use std::path::Path;\n11: \n12: use serde::Deserialize;\n13: \n14: // Embed theme YAML files at compile time\n15: pub const DEFAULT_DARK_YAML: &str = include_str!(\"../themes/dark.yaml\");\n16: pub const FLEET_DARK_YAML: &str = include_str!(\"../themes/fleet-dark.yaml\");\n17: pub const GITHUB_DARK_YAML: &str = include_str!(\"../themes/github-dark.yaml\");\n18: pub const GITHUB_LIGHT_YAML: &str = include_str!(\"../themes/github-light.yaml\");\n19: \n20: /// A built-in theme entry\n21: pub struct BuiltinTheme {\n22:     /// Stable identifier for config (e.g. \"default-dark\", \"fleet-dark\")\n23:     pub id: &'static str,\n24:     /// Embedded YAML content\n25:     pub yaml: &'static str,\n26: }\n27: \n28: /// Registry of all built-in themes\n29: pub const BUILTIN_THEMES: &[BuiltinTheme] = &[\n30:     BuiltinTheme {\n31:         id: \"default-dark\",\n32:         yaml: DEFAULT_DARK_YAML,\n33:     },\n34:     BuiltinTheme {\n35:         id: \"fleet-dark\",\n36:         yaml: FLEET_DARK_YAML,\n37:     },\n38:     BuiltinTheme {\n39:         id: \"github-dark\",\n40:         yaml: GITHUB_DARK_YAML,\n41:     },\n42:     BuiltinTheme {\n43:         id: \"github-light\",\n44:         yaml: GITHUB_LIGHT_YAML,\n45:     },\n46: ];\n47: \n48: /// Where the theme came from\n49: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]\n50: pub enum ThemeSource {\n51:     /// User-defined theme in ~/.config/token-editor/themes/\n52:     User,\n53:     /// Built-in theme embedded in binary\n54:     Builtin,\n55: }\n56: \n57: /// Information about an available theme\n58: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n59: pub struct ThemeInfo {\n60:     /// Stable identifier (e.g., \"default-dark\", \"my-custom-theme\")\n61:     pub id: String,\n62:     /// Display name from YAML (e.g., \"Default Dark\")\n63:     pub name: String,\n64:     /// Where this theme is loaded from\n65:     pub source: ThemeSource,\n66: }\n67: \n68: /// Load a theme from a YAML file\n69: pub fn from_file(path: &Path) -> Result<Theme, String> {\n70:     let content = std::fs::read_to_string(path)\n71:         .map_err(|e| format!(\"Failed to read theme file {}: {}\", path.display(), e))?;\n72:     Theme::from_yaml(&content)\n73: }\n74: \n75: /// Load theme by id with priority: user → builtin\n76: ///\n77: /// Searches in order:\n78: /// 1. `~/.config/token-editor/themes/{id}.yaml`\n79: /// 2. Embedded builtin themes\n80: pub fn load_theme(id: &str) -> Result<Theme, String> {\n81:     // Try user themes directory\n82:     if let Some(user_dir) = crate::config_paths::themes_dir() {\n83: &EditorMsg, is_editing: bool) -> Option<CsvMsg> {\n104:     match (editor_msg, is_editing) {\n105:         // When editing, left/right move cursor within cell\n106:         (EditorMsg::MoveCursor(Direction::Left), true) => Some(CsvMsg::EditCursorLeft),\n107:         (EditorMsg::MoveCursor(Direction::Right), true) => Some(CsvMsg::EditCursorRight),\n108:         // When editing, up/down confirm edit and navigate\n109:         (EditorMsg::MoveCursor(Direction::Up), true) => Some(CsvMsg::ConfirmEditUp),\n110:         (EditorMsg::MoveCursor(Direction::Down), true) => Some(CsvMsg::ConfirmEdit),\n111:         // When editing, Home/End move cursor within cell\n112:         (EditorMsg::MoveCursorLineStart, true) => Some(CsvMsg::EditCursorHome),\n113:         (EditorMsg::MoveCursorLineEnd, true) => Some(CsvMsg::EditCursorEnd),\n114: \n115:         // When not editing, standard cell navigation\n116:         (EditorMsg::MoveCursor(Direction::Up), false) => Some(CsvMsg::MoveUp),\n117:         (EditorMsg::MoveCursor(Direction::Down), false) => Some(CsvMsg::MoveDown),\n118:         (EditorMsg::MoveCursor(Direction::Left), false) => Some(CsvMsg::MoveLeft),\n119:         (EditorMsg::MoveCursor(Direction::Right), false) => Some(CsvMsg::MoveRight),\n120:         (EditorMsg::MoveCursorLineStart, false) => Some(CsvMsg::RowStart),\n121:         (EditorMsg::MoveCursorLineEnd, false) => Some(CsvMsg::RowEnd),\n122:         (EditorMsg::MoveCursorDocumentStart, _) => Some(CsvMsg::FirstCell),\n123:         (EditorMsg::MoveCursorDocumentEnd, _) => Some(CsvMsg::LastCell),\n124:         (EditorMsg::PageUp, _) => Some(CsvMsg::PageUp),\n125:         (EditorMsg::PageDown, _) => Some(CsvMsg::PageDown),\n126:         _ => None,\n127:     }\n128: }\n129: \n130: /// Map document messages to CSV cell editing messages\n131: ///\n132: /// When not editing: InsertNewline starts editing, InsertChar starts with that char\n133: /// When editing: InsertNewline confirms edit, InsertChar inserts into buffer\n134: fn map_document_to_csv(doc_msg: &DocumentMsg, is_editing: bool) -> Option<CsvMsg> {\n135:     match (doc_msg, is_editing) {\n136:         (DocumentMsg::InsertNewline, false) => Some(CsvMsg::StartEditing),\n137:         (DocumentMsg::InsertNewline, true) => Some(CsvMsg::ConfirmEdit),\n138:         (DocumentMsg::InsertChar(ch), false) => Some(CsvMsg::StartEditingWithChar(*ch)),\n139:         (DocumentMsg::InsertChar(ch), true) => Some(CsvMsg::EditInsertChar(*ch)),\n140:         (DocumentMsg::DeleteBackward, true) => Some(CsvMsg::EditDeleteBackward),\n141:         (DocumentMsg::DeleteForward, true) => Some(CsvMsg::EditDeleteForward),\n142:         _ => None,\n143:     }\n144: }\n145: \n146: /// Traced update wrapper (debug builds only)\n147: ///\n148: /// Captures before/after cursor state and logs diffs for debugging.\n149: /// Filters out noisy messages like BlinkCursor from logging.\n150: #[cfg(debug_assertions)]\n151: fn update_traced(model: &mut AppModel, msg: Msg) -> Option<Cmd> {\n152:     use crate::messages::UiMsg;\n153: \n154:     // Skip logging for noisy periodic messages\n155:     let is_noisy = matches!(&msg, Msg::Ui(UiMsg::BlinkCursor));\n156: \n157:     let msg_name = msg_type_name(&msg);\n158:     let _span = if is_noisy {\n159:         None\n160:     } else {\n161:         Some(span!(Level::DEBUG, \"update\", msg = %msg_name).entered())\n162:     };\n163: \n164:     let before = model.focused_editor().map(CursorSnapshot::from_editor);\n165: \n166:     if !is_noisy {\n167:         debug!(target: \"message\", msg = %msg_name, \"processing\");\n168:     }\n169: \n170:     let result = update_inner(model, msg.clone());\n171: \n172:     let diff = if let (Some(ref before), Some(editor)) = (&before, model.focused_editor()) {\n173:         let after = editor.assert_invariants_with_context(&msg_name);\n185:     }\n186: \n187:     if !is_noisy {\n188:         if let Some(ref mut overlay) = model.debug_overlay {\n189:             overlay.record_message(msg_name.clone(), diff);\n190:         }\n191:     }\n192: \n193:     result\n194: }\n195: \n196: /// Get a display name for a message type\n197: ///\n198: /// Uses Debug formatting to include variant names and arguments.\n199: /// Example outputs:\n200: /// - `Editor::MoveCursor(Up)`\n201: /// - `Document::InsertChar('x')`\n202: /// - `App::Resize(1920, 1080)`\n203: #[cfg(debug_assertions)]\n204: fn msg_type_name(msg: &Msg) -> String {\n205:     match msg {\n206:         Msg::Editor(m) => format!(\"Editor::{:?}\", m),\n207:         Msg::Document(m) =>   "path": "~/code/token-editor/src/markdown/theme.rs",
  "content": "//! Preview theme - colors for Markdown preview rendering\n\nuse crate::theme::{Color, Theme};\n\n/// Theme colors for markdown preview (CSS-formatted)\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct PreviewTheme {\n    pub background: String,\n    pub text: String,\n    pub heading: String,\n    pub link: String,\n    pub code_background: String,\n    pub border: String,\n    pub accent: String,\n    pub muted: String,\n}\n\nimpl PreviewTheme {\n    /// Generate preview theme from editor theme\n    pub fn from_editor_theme(theme: &Theme) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            background: color_to_css(&theme.editor.background),\n            text: color_to_css(&theme.editor.foreground),\n            heading: color_to_css(&theme.syntax.keyword),\n            link: color_to_css(&theme.syntax.string),\n            code_background: color_to_css(&theme.gutter.background),\n            border: color_to_css(&theme.gutter.border_color),\n            accent: color_to_css(&theme.syntax.function),\n            muted: color_to_css(&theme.gutter.foreground),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nimpl Default for PreviewTheme {\n    fn default() -> Self {\n        Self {\n            background: \"#1e1e1e\".to_string(),\n            text: \"#d4d4d4\".to_string(),\n            heading: \"#569cd6\".to_string(),\n            link: \"#ce9178\".to_string(),\n            code_background: \"#252526\".to_string(),\n            border: \"#3c3c3c\".to_string(),\n            accent: \"#dcdcaa\".to_string(),\n            muted: \"#858585\".to_string(),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nfn color_to_css(color: &Color) -> String {\n    if color.a == 255 {\n        format!(\"#{:02x}{:02x}{:02x}\", color.r, color.g, color.b)\n    } else {\n        format!(\n            \"#{:02x}{:02x}{:02x}{:02x}\",\n            color.r, color.g, color.b, color.a\n        )\n    }\n}\n"
}

styling\npub fn markdown_to_html(markdown: &str, theme: &PreviewTheme) -> String {\n    let options = Options::ENABLE_TABLES\n        | Options::ENABLE_FOOTNOTES\n        | Options::ENABLE_STRIKETHROUGH\n        | Options::ENABLE_TASKLISTS;\n\n    let parser = Parser::new_ext(markdown, options);\n\n    // Add line markers for scroll sync\n    let parser_with_markers = add_line_markers(parser, markdown);\n\n    let mut html_output = String::new();\n    html::push_html(&mut html_output, parser_with_markers);\n\n    format!(\n        r#\"<!DOCTYPE html>\n<html>\n<head>\n    <meta charset=\"utf-8\">\n    <meta name=\"viewport\" content=\"width=device-width, initial-scale=1\">\n    <style>{}</style>\n</head>\n<body>\n    <div generate_css(theme: &PreviewTheme) -> String {\n    format!(\n        r#\"\n* {{\n    box-sizing: border-box;\n}}\n\nbody {{\n    font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, \"Segoe UI\", Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif;\n    font-size: 14px;\n    line-height: 1.6;\n    color: {text};\n    background: {background};\n    padding: 20px;\n    max-width: 800px;\n    margin: 0 auto;\n}}\n\nh1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6 {{\n    color: {heading};\n    margin-top: 24px;\n    margin-bottom: 16px;\n    font-weight: 600;\n    line-height: 1.25;\n}}\n\nh1 {{\n    font-size: 2em;\n    border-bottom: 1px solid {border};\n    padding-bottom: 0.3em;\n}}\n\nh2 {{\n    font-size: 1.5em;\n    border-bottom: 1px solid {border};\n    padding-bottom: 0.3em;\n}}\n\nh3 {{\n    font-size: 1.25em;\n}}\n\nh4 {{\n    font-size: 1em;\n}}\n\nh5 {{\n    font-size: 0.875em;\n}}\n\nh6 {{\n    font-size: 0.85em;\n    color: {muted};\n}}\n\np {{\n    margin-top: 0;\n    margin-bottom: 16px;\n}}\n\ncode {{\n    background: {code_background};\n    padding: 0.2em 0.4em;\n    border-radius: 3px;\n    font-family: \"SF Mono\", \"Fira Code\", Consolas, \"Liberation Mono\", Menlo, Courier, monospace;\n    font-size: 0.9em;\n}}\n\npre {{\n    background: {code_background};\n    padding: 16px;\n    border-radius: 6px;\n    overflow-x: auto;\n    margin-top: 0;\n    margin-bottom: 16px;\n}}\n\npre code {{\n    background: none;\n    padding: 0;\n    font-size: 0.875em;\n    line-height: 1.45;\n}}\n\nblockquote {{\n    border-left: 4px solid {accent};\n    margin: 0 0 16px 0;\n    padding: 0 16px;\n    color: {muted};\n}}\n\nblockquote > :first-child {{\n    margin-top: 0;\n}}\n\nblockquote > :last-child {{\n    margin-bottom: 0;\n}}\n\na {{\n    color: {link};\n    text-decoration: none;\n}}\n\na:hover {{\n    text-decoration: underline;\n}}\n\nul, ol {{\n    padding-left: 2em;\n    margin-top: 0;\n    margin-bottom: 16px;\n}}\n\nli {{\n    margin-bottom: 0.25em;\n}}\n\nli > p {{\n    margin-top: 16px;\n}}\n\nli + li {{\n    margin-top: 0.25em;\n}}\n\nhr {{\n    height: 0.25em;\n    padding: 0;\n    margin: 24px 0;\n    background-color: {border};\n    border: 0;\n}}\n\ntable {{\n    border-collapse: collapse;\n    border-spacing: 0;\n    margin-bottom: 16px;\n    width: 100%;\n    overflow: auto;\n}}\n\nth, td {{\n    padding: 6px 13px;\n    border: 1px solid {border};\n}}\n\nth {{\n    font-weight: 600;\n    background: {code_background};\n}}\n\ntr:nth-child(2n) {{\n    background: {code_background};\n}}\n\nimg {{\n    max-width: 100%;\n    box-sizing: content-box;\n}}\n\ninput[type=\"checkbox\"] {{\n    margin-right: 0.5em;\n}}\n\n.task-list-item {{\n    list-style-type: none;\n}}\n\n.task-list-item + .task-list-item {{\n    margin-top: 3px;\n}}\n\n.task-list-item input {{\n    margin: 0 0.2em 0.25em -1.6em;\n    vertical-align: middle;\n}}\n\ndel {{\n    color: {muted};\n}}\n\n[data-line] {{\n    scroll-margin-top: 20px;\n}}\n\"#,\n        text = theme.text,\n        background = theme.background,\n        heading = theme.heading,\n        link = theme.link,\n        code_background = theme.code_background,\n        border = theme.border,\n        accent = theme.accent,\n        muted = theme.muted,\n    )\n}\n\n/// JavaScript for scroll synchronization\nconst SCROLL_SYNC_JS: &str = r#\"\n// Scroll to a specific source line\nwindow.scrollToLine = function(line) {\n    const el = document.querySelector(`[data-line=\"${line}\"]`);\n    if (el) {\n        el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });\n    }\n};\n\n// Report scroll position back to editor\nlet scrollTimeout = null;\nwindow.addEventListener('scroll', function() {\n    if (scrollTimeout) clearTimeout(scrollTimeout);\n    scrollTimeout = setTimeout(function() {\n        const elements = document.querySelectorAll('[data-line]');\n        let visibleLine = null;\n        const viewportTop = window.scrollY;\n        \n        for (const el of elements) {\n            const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();\n            if (rect.top >= 0) {\n                visibleLine = parseInt(el.getAttribute('data-line'), 10);\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n        \n        if (visibleLine !== null && window.webkit && window.webkit.messageHandlers) {\n            window.webkit.messageHandlers.scrollSync.postMessage({ line: visibleLine });\n        }\n    }, 100);\n});\n\"#;\n\n/// Add data-line attributes to block-level elements for scroll sync\nfn add_line_markers<'a>(\n    parser: Parser<'a, 'a>,\n    markdown: &'a str,\n) -> impl Iterator<Item = Event<'a>> {\n    let mut current_line = 1;\n    let mut last_offset = 0;\n\n    parser.into_offset_iter().flat_map(move |(event, range)| {\n        // Update line number based on offset\n        let new_lines = markdown[last_offset..range.start]\n            .chars()\n            .filter(|c| *c == '\\n')\n            .count();\n        current_line += new_lines;\n        last_offset = range.start;\n\n        match &event {\n            Event::Start(tag) => {\n                let line = current_line;\n                match tag {\n                    Tag::Heading { .. }\n                    | Tag::Paragraph\n                    | Tag::BlockQuote(_)\n                    | Tag::CodeBlock(_)\n                    | Tag::List(_)\n                    | Tag::Item => {\n                        // Insert a span with data-line before the element\n                        vec![\n                            Event::Html(format!(r#\"<span data-line=\"{}\"></span>\"#, line).into()),\n                            event,\n                        ]\n                    }\n                    _ => vec![event],\n                }\n            }\n            Event::End(TagEnd::Heading(_))\n            | Event::End(TagEnd::Paragraph)\n            | Event::End(TagEnd::BlockQuote(_))\n            | Event::End(TagEnd::CodeBlock)\n            | Event::End(TagEnd::List(_))\n            | Event::End(TagEnd::Item) => vec![event],\n            _ => vec![event],\n        }\n    })\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_markdown_to_html_basic() {\n        let md = \"# Hello\\n\\nWorld\";\n        let theme = PreviewTheme::default();\n        let html = markdown_to_html(md, &theme);\n\n        assert!(html.contains(\"<h1>\"));\n        assert!(html.contains(\"Hello\"));\n        assert!(html.contains(\"<p>\"));\n        assert!(html.contains(\"World\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_markdown_to_html_code_block() {\n        let md = \"```rust\\nfn main() {}\\n```\";\n        let theme = PreviewTheme::default();\n        let html = markdown_to_html(md, &theme);\n\n        assert!(html.contains(\"<pre>\"));\n        assert!(html.contains(\"<code\"));\n        assert!(html.contains(\"fn main()\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_markdown_to_html_links() {\n        let md = \"[Click here](https://example.com)\";\n        let theme = PreviewTheme::default();\n        let html = markdown_to_html(md, &theme);\n\n        assert!(html.contains(\"<a\"));\n        assert!(html.contains(\"href=\\\"https://example.com\\\"\"));\n        assert!(html.contains(\"Click here\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_markdown_to_html_tables() {\n        let md = \"| A | B |\\n|---|---|\\n| 1 | 2 |\";\n        let theme = PreviewTheme::default();\n        let html = markdown_to_html(md, &theme);\n\n        assert!(html.contains(\"<table>\"));\n        assert!(html.contains(\"<th>\"));\n        assert!(html.contains(\"<td>\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_preview_theme_default() {\n        let theme = PreviewTheme::default();\n        assert!(!theme.background.is_empty());\n        assert!(!theme.text.is_empty());\n        assert!(theme.background.starts_with('#'));\n    }\n}\n"
}

Markdown preview pane state\n\nuse crate::model::editor_area::{DocumentId, Rect};\n\n/// Unique identifier for a preview pane\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]\npub struct PreviewPaneId(pub u64);\n\n/// State for a markdown preview pane\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct PreviewPane {\n    pub id: PreviewPaneId,\n    pub document_id: DocumentId,\n    pub rendered_html: String,\n    pub rendered_lines: Vec<RenderedLine>,\n    pub scroll_offset: usize,\n    pub scroll_sync_enabled: bool,\n    pub rect: Rect,\n    pub last_revision: u64,\n}\n\n/// A rendered line in the preview (for native text rendering)\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct RenderedLine {\n    pub segments: Vec<StyledSegment>,\n    pub source_line: usize,\n}\n\n/// A styled segment of text\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct StyledSegment {\n    pub text: String,\n    pub style: MarkdownStyle,\n}\n\n/// Style variants for markdown elements\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]\npub enum MarkdownStyle {\n    Normal,\n    Heading1,\n    Heading2,\n    Heading3,\n    Heading4,\n    Heading5,\n    Heading6,\n    Bold,\n    Italic,\n    BoldItalic,\n    Code,\n    CodeBlock,\n    Link,\n    ListBullet,\n    ListNumber,\n    BlockquoteMarker,\n    BlockquoteText,\n    HorizontalRule,\n}\n\nimpl PreviewPane {\n    pub fn new(id: PreviewPaneId, document_id: DocumentId) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            id,\n            document_id,\n            rendered_html: String::new(),\n            rendered_lines: Vec::new(),\n            scroll_offset: 0,\n            scroll_sync_enabled: true,\n            rect: Rect::default(),\n            last_revision: 0,\n        }\n    }\n\n    pub fn needs_refresh(&self, document_revision: u64) -> bool {\n        self.last_revision != document_revision\n    }\n}\n"

## Assistant

The core implementation is complete. Let me provide a summary of what was implemented:

### Summary

I've implemented the **Markdown Preview** feature (F-170) for Token Editor. Here's what was built:

#### Phase 1: Dependencies & Core Module
- Added `pulldown-cmark` (0.12) and `wry` (0.50) to [Cargo.toml](file://~/code/token-editor/Cargo.toml#L55-L56)
- Created [src/markdown/](file://~/code/token-editor/src/markdown/) module with:
  - [renderer.rs](file://~/code/token-editor/src/markdown/renderer.rs) - Markdown to HTML conversion with scroll sync markers
  - [theme.rs](file://~/code/token-editor/src/markdown/theme.rs) - Preview theme colors from editor theme
  - [preview.rs](file://~/code/token-editor/src/markdown/preview.rs) - PreviewPane state management

#### Phase 2: Layout Integration
- Added `LayoutNode::Preview` variant to [editor_area.rs](file://~/code/token-editor/src/model/editor_area.rs#L136-L142)
- Added `PreviewId` type and preview pane storage to `EditorArea`
- Implemented preview management methods:
  - `open_preview_for_document()` - Creates split with preview pane
  - `close_preview()` - Removes preview and collapses split
  - `toggle_focused_preview()` - Toggle preview for current document
- Added `PreviewMsg` enum to [messages.rs](file://~/code/token-editor/src/messages.rs#L400-L417)
- Added [update/preview.rs](file://~/code/token-editor/src/update/preview.rs) handler

#### Phase 3: Rendering & Keybindings
- Added native markdown preview rendering in [view/mod.rs](file://~/code/token-editor/src/view/mod.rs#L536-L672):
  - Header bar with "Preview" title
  - Basic markdown styling (headings, code blocks, lists, links)
- Added commands in [keymap/command.rs](file://~/code/token-editor/src/keymap/command.rs#L224-L230):
  - `MarkdownTogglePreview`
  - `MarkdownOpenPreviewToSide`
- Added keybinding `Cmd+Shift+V` to toggle preview in [keymap.yaml](file://~/code/token-editor/keymap.yaml#L330-L334)

#### What Works
- Press `Cmd+Shift+V` on any file to open a side-by-side preview pane
- Preview shows basic markdown styling (headings in keyword color, bullets, code blocks with background)
- Press `Cmd+Shift+V` again to close the preview
- Split resizing via splitter bars works

#### Remaining Work (future phases)
- **Scroll synchronization** between source and preview
- **Auto-refresh** on document edits (with debouncing)
- **Close preview** when switching to non-markdown file
- Enhanced rendering (bold/italic, nested lists, tables)
- Webview-based rendering for full HTML/CSS support (optional)
